# Appendix — every UI string mapped to the section that covers it

Generated by `assemble.py`: a string is covered when its i18n key appears in a section, or its normalised text appears there (substring for 4+ characters, whole word below that). Matching is per section — never across file boundaries.

| Catalog | Strings | Covered |
|---|---:|---:|
| desktop_en | 2354 | 2354 (100%) |
| gateway_locale_en | 367 | 367 (100%) |
| tui_content | 137 | 137 (100%) |
| web_en | 656 | 656 (100%) |
| web_live | 1205 | 1205 (100%) |
